    const clean = text.replace(/\s+/g, " ").slice(0, 600);
    const tts = ttsP ? await ttsP : null;
    if (tts) { try { stateEl.innerHTML = ""; stateEl.append(t("aiSpeaking")); stateEl.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", '<span class="wave"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>'); await tts.speak(clean, { language: lang === "es" ? "es" : "en" }); } catch (e) { browserSpeak(clean); } finally { stateEl.textContent = t("aiOnline"); } return; }
    browserSpeak(clean);
  }
  function browserSpeak(text) {
    if (!("speechSynthesis" in window)) return;
    try {
      speechSynthesis.cancel(); const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text); u.lang = lang === "es" ? "es-US" : "en-US"; u.rate = 1.02;
      const vs = speechSynthesis.getVoices(); const v = vs.find((x) => x.lang && x.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(lang) && /natural|premium|enhanced|google|paulina|m[oó]nica|samantha|ava/i.test(x.name)) || vs.find((x) => x.lang && x.lang.toLowerCase().startsWith(lang)); if (v) u.voice = v;
      u.onstart = () => { stateEl.textContent = t("aiSpeaking"); stateEl.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", '<span class="wave"><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>'); };
      u.onend = u.onerror = () => { stateEl.textContent = t("aiOnline"); };
      speechSynthesis.speak(u);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  function stopVoice() { try { speechSynthesis.cancel(); } catch (e) {} if (ttsP) ttsP.then((x) => { try { x && x.stop(); } catch (e) {} }); }
  // the local brain: answers from the business facts when Claude is not available to this viewer
  function local(q) {
    const s = q.toLowerCase().normalize("NFD").replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, "");
    let best = null, score = 0;
    for (const item of KB) { let sc = 0; for (const w of item.k) if (s.includes(w)) sc += w.length > 4 ? 2 : 1; if (sc > score) { score = sc; best = item; } }
    return best && score > 0 ? best[lang] : t("aiFallback");
  }
  async function ask(q) {
    q = q.trim(); if (!q || busy) return; busy = true; add("you", q); history.push({ role: "user", content: q }); input.value = "";

    let answer = null;
    if (sample) {
      try {

No inventes precios exactos; ofrece el estimado gratis y llamar o enviar un texto al 954-549-5176. Nunca menciones WhatsApp.\n\nDatos del negocio:\n" + KB.map((x) => "- " + x.es).join("\n");

        const r = await sample(msgs, { modelTier: "quick", cache: false, onText: ({ text }) => { typing.classList.remove("typing"); typing.textContent = text; log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight; } });
        answer = (r && r.text || "").trim() || null;
      } catch (e) { answer = null; }
    }
    if (!answer) { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 450)); answer = local(q); }
    typing.remove(); bot(answer, true); busy = false;
  }
  $("#ai-form").addEventListener("submit", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); ask(input.value); });
  $("#ai-mic").addEventListener("click", () => {

    }

  if (!reduce && window.gsap) {
    const h = $(".hero h1");
    const wrapWords = (node) => { [...node.childNodes].forEach((n) => { if (n.nodeType === 3) { const f = document.createDocumentFragment(); n.nodeValue.split(/(\s+)/).forEach((w) => { if (!w) return; if (/^\s+$/.test(w)) f.append(w); else { const sp = document.createElement("span"); sp.className = "w"; sp.textContent = w; f.append(sp); } }); n.replaceWith(f); } else if (n.nodeType === 1) wrapWords(n); }); };
    wrapWords(h);
    gsap.from(h.querySelectorAll(".w"), { yPercent: 90, opacity: 0, rotate: 1.5, duration: 1.3, ease: "expo.out", stagger: 0.075, delay: 1.7, clearProps: "all" });
  }
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
